--- Commit Message ---
LU-19712 utils: Handle migrate/mirror on pinned files

Add a safety gate to lfs setstripe/migrate/mirror operations that
respects a file's lustre.pin pool constraints:

- migrate requires the new layout to keep a pinned pool's coverage:
  a whole mirror on the pool for a plain pin, every component for an
  exclusive pin, and none on a denied pool. A flat/omitted --pool
  falls back to the pool the MDS would itself assign from the target
  directory's default layout, so an in-place restripe still
  succeeds; a bare "lfs migrate FILE" that keeps the file's own
  layout is exempted entirely, since nothing about pool placement
  can change. MDT-only migrate (-m) is not covered.
- mirror extend and component-add only add data alongside what is
  already there, so only an exclusive pin or a deny_pool constraint
  restrict the new components; a plain pin is satisfied by the
  untouched existing copy.
- mirror split and mirror delete are rejected only if removing the
  targeted mirror would leave a pinned pool with no surviving copy.
- mirror create is not covered: it always targets a file with no
  existing layout.
- DoM and foreign components carry no OST pool and are skipped.

mirror split/delete's selector and the pin gate's survivor check now
share one resolve_split_mirror_id() helper, so they can't disagree
about which mirror is targeted.

Also add llapi_pool_is_denied_fd()/_file()/_fid() to
include/lustre/lustreapi.h alongside the existing is_pinned trio,
with a matching man3 page, and have the gate parse each file's
lustre.pin xattr once per call instead of once per layout component.

Signed-off-by: Nikos Papakonstantinou <npapakonstantinou@ddn.com>

I attempted to fix the build errors present in this patch and succeeded. Yay! Now, keep in mind I barely have any idea what I am doing, so please doublecheck the changes I made to make sure they make sense.

Here's summary of my changes:
## Fix Explanation

**What was fixed:**
I removed the unused variable declaration `char *p;` from line 6018 in `lustre/utils/lfs.c`.

**Rationale:**
The compile error occurred because:
1. The compiler flag `-Werror=unused-variable` treats unused variable warnings as errors
2. The variable `char *p;` was declared but never referenced anywhere in the code block
3. This variable appears to have been left over from development or was intended for future use but never implemented

The fix simply removes this unused variable declaration. Looking at the code logic, there's no need for this variable - the code uses `strstr()` to search for specific patterns in `pinbuf`, and the return values from `strstr()` are only used for conditional checks (truthiness), not for any string manipulation that would require storing a pointer.

--- Message (Gerrit AI review for Lustre, 2026-08-28 14:21:47.000000000) ---
Patch Set 32:

(6 comments)

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Some concerns, see inline comments. The new pool-less migrate fallback (migrate_target_pool_get()) is the piece of this patch with the most behaviour packed into it, and sanity test_157i never reaches it: every migrate in the test is either a bare 'lfs migrate' (which takes the from_copy shortcut and re-applies the file's own layout) or a '--pool'-qualified one. Would it be worth adding a case like 'lfs setstripe --pool P -c 1 f; lfs pool pin --pool P --file f; lfs migrate -c 2 f' so both the allow and the refuse side of that fallback are covered?
